**Per-shot prompt (paste-ready):**

```
Cinematic photoreal still frame, low-angle tracking shot along a wet asphalt access road
leading to a white industrial data center building under soft overcast daylight. Chain-link
fence and red laterite dirt embankment blur past on the left under motion streaks, dense
tropical foliage blurs past on the right, both foreground edges compressed with directional
motion blur suggesting forward camera speed. Centered on the road surface, a long-exposure
red light trail — rendered as two to three thin glowing fiber-optic strands twisted loosely
together, each carrying small bright white-hot sparkle points scattered along its length —
starts tight and saturated in the extreme foreground bottom-left, then sweeps in a shallow
rising diagonal along the road's own vanishing perspective, thinning, dimming, and converging
toward a single point near the guardhouse and boom barrier at the entrance gate in the
mid-distance. The light trail hugs the road's actual surface and camber rather than floating
above it, kicks up fine dust/gravel particulate around its brightest near-camera segment, and
fades to a faint red glow by the time it reaches the gate — it does not extend past the gate
or into the building itself. Wet asphalt reflects a soft red glow directly beneath the trail.
Deep focus on the building and gate, motion-blur only on the frame edges and the light trail
itself. Cool silver-grey sky, muted natural color palette overall, with the red trail as the
single saturated color accent. Cinematic sci-fi tech/energy establishing shot, photoreal,
grounded, no fantasy elements.

Negative: no warping of the building facade or fence lines, no duplicate or branching light
trails, no light trail floating above the road surface, no trail extending past the entrance
gate, no added text or logos, no lens flare on the building itself, no change to sky color or
time of day, no extra vehicles or people, trail stays a coherent continuous ribbon rather than
scattered fragments.
```

# Shot Prompt — Image-to-Video Pipeline Runner

Runs this project's pipeline for one shot, start to finish, instead of the user (or a fresh Claude turn) re-deriving it by hand each time.

## Ground

**PRE:** …ft/motion-blurred throughout rather than a crisp still — noted as a Stage 1 weakness for locked-element fidelity, but it actually works here since it already reads as "mid-motion."

Paste-ready prompt (1499 characters, positive + negative):

```
Cinematic wide shot, high-tech control-room mood, red #E4002B as the only saturated color against cool dark steel and desaturated blue-white screen glow, wide-angle perspective, photoreal commercial finish, 16:9. Continuity: the red light-network trail is the anchor, its color and travel direction fixed.

Camera whip-pans hard to the right, chasing the red light trail at its own velocity, heavy directional motion blur building through the pan.
Beat 1 (0-1s): frame holds on the control room as thin red light strands converge near the foreground desks, pulsing and gathering into a single bright trail.
Beat 2 (1-3s): camera snaps into a full-speed whip pan right, tracking the light as it streaks across the room past the video wall, the trail stretching into a hard red smear, operators and desks reduced to blurred vertical streaks.
Beat 3 (3-4s): the light trail clears the right edge and exits frame completely, motion blur carrying a half-beat further before the pan eases, ending on empty streaked motion.

Locks: ceiling structure, video-wall bezels, desk architecture stay rigid, parallax/blur only, no warping. No legible text/UI/logos on any screen. Light trail color and brightness match its close-up reference throughout.

Negative: no flicker/strobing beyond the light trail itself, no colors outside red #E4002B plus neutral cool greys, no warping on ceiling/video-wall/desks, no legible text/UI/logos/signage, no direction change or stutter mid-pan, pan doesn't reverse or stall.
```

# Shot Prompt — Image-to-Video Pipeline Runner

Runs this project's pipeline for one shot, start to finish, instead of the user (or a fresh Claude turn) re-deriving it by hand each time.

## Per-shot prompt (paste-ready)

```
Camera cranes smoothly and slowly straight upward through open space, lifting the eyeline so more of the valley and skyline enter frame. Deep focus, sharp from foreground grass to the far skyline.
Beat 1 (0-2s): a single bright red light stream draws itself in from the lower-left foreground beside the girl (point A), sweeping low past her left side and out across the valley, streaking and brightening as it extends.
Beat 2 (2-4s): the girl turns her head to her left to follow it as it passes; her hair lifts and drifts slowly in a gentle wind; the other red trails pulse faintly along their paths.
Beat 3 (4-5s): the stream reaches the skyline and converges into the city on the bay (point B) in a bright knot; the camera settles at the top of its rise, hair still trailing, grass swaying.
Warm hazy daylight, faint sun glare, thin haze over the city, red #E4002B and white light the only saturated color against the desaturated green-gold hilltop and hazy skyline.
Locks: skyline, bayfront hotel towers, domed gardens, observation wheel and bridges stay rigid and unchanged; picnic blanket and basket stay put; the hero light stays one continuous ribbon; girl's dress and bow do not warp; horizon stays level.
```

Warm daylight, neutral tones, no saturated red, normal focal length, photoreal finish, 16:9, shallow focus on his face. # Shot Prompt — Image-to-Video Pipeline Runner

Runs this project's pipeline for one shot, start to finish, instead of the user (or a fresh Claude turn) re-deriving it by hand each time.

## Ground

**PRE:** …ference, not racked into. Fallback if bench edges shear: drop to Slider Left.

**Positive prompt (Kling — 1180 chars):**

```
Slow shallow arc left around a seated research scientist, over-the-shoulder framing, telephoto compression, shallow depth of field, 16:9, no red light-network.
The camera arcs slowly left the whole shot, about 20 degrees, from over her right shoulder to behind her, turning the centre monitor toward head-on, then eases to rest; foreground labware slides in parallax.
Beat 1 (0-2s): her hand on the mouse makes one small adjustment; a new window begins opening on the centre monitor; cool screen glow lifts on her face.
Beat 2 (2-4.5s): the window expands and a blue-helix, gold-sheet protein ribbon structure resolves and fills the centre screen; she leans slightly toward it.
Beat 3 (4.5-6s): the structure settles, no spinning, no morphing; the arc comes to rest.
Beat 4 (6-8.5s): she picks up the disposable cup, one slow sip, lowers it.
Beat 5 (8.5-10s): a slow blink and a few-degree head turn toward camera; camera locked.
Locks: monitors, stands, bench, keyboard, pipette and tube racks, petri dish stay rigid, parallax only; side monitors stay an unreadable soft glow; on-screen content never resolves into legible text, UI or logos; the cup stays put until she lifts it.
```

### Kling — positive (1134) + negative (359) = 1493/1500

```
Cinematic slow dolly-in over a golden-hour rice field from the air, high vantage, deep focus, 16:9. Camera creeps straight forward at constant height, no descent or tilt; near bunds parallax past, treeline and horizon fixed.
Beat 1 (0-4s): open on the plain field; a translucent green scan wedge sweeps from horizon centre, fanning across the paddies to the lower-right; the HUD fades in - "NDVI Avg: 0.61" top-left, "Soil Moisture: 34%" bottom-left, "Crop Health: 87%" bottom-right - digits tick up once, then settle exactly.

The red emission casts a soft crimson glow onto the cable jacket, the surrounding sand, and the water immediately around it, with a faint red volumetric haze in the beam of the sunrays. Cinematic wide establishing photograph of a modern data-center campus plaza at golden hour. A lone construction worker in a dark navy work shirt, dark trousers and a white hard hat walks right-to-left across the empty foreground plaza, mid-stride at an unhurried pace, both hands empty and relaxed, one arm bent in a natural walking swing, a long soft shadow cast toward the camera. He is small in frame, lower-left third.

Dominating the right two-thirds of the frame: a monumental building clad in vertical bronze/copper anodized fin louvres, warm rim-lit by a low sun, with dark recessed glass, a cantilevered entrance canopy of angled glass awnings, and a grey granite plinth. The building corner sits about 60% across the frame in strong two-point perspective, receding to the right. Behind it at left: a pale grey ribbed industrial building and faint silhouettes of construction cranes and scaffolding. Three limp flags on tall flagpoles at mid-left. A receding row of slender young trees in tree-grates along grey paving; two pop-up lawn sprinklers throw fans of fine backlit mist over the trees. Low sun behind the building at upper-left with hazy pale sky, gentle lens flare and halation, warm backlit facade against a cooler plaza foreground.

Eye-level camera, wide-angle lens, low horizon, deep focus sharp from foreground paving to background. Shot on ARRI Alexa 35, fine natural film grain, filmic highlight rolloff, natural colour. Natural golden-hour neutrals only — no saturated accent colours, no red. 16:9.

Negative: anything held in the worker's hands, coffee cup, bottle, phone, bag, tool; text, numerals, signage, logos, watermark, UI; warped or rippling facade, shimmering fins, morphing cranes; extra limbs, distorted face, duplicated figures; blown highlights; red or magenta accents; CGI or 3D-render look, over-sharpened noise-free plastic image, HDR look.
```

# Shot Prompt — Image-to-Video Pipeline Runner

Runs this project's pipeline for one shot, start to finish, instead of the user (or a fresh Claude turn) re-deriving it by hand each time.

## Ground

**PRE:** … the clip." No timecoding (no editorial cut beat to hit); the staggered emergence is handled with "not all at once" + the QC restatement below. Not a continuation of an approved clip → no match‑cut opening.

---

## Paste-ready prompt (10s)

```
Cinematic wide exterior shot of the white data-centre building, premium data-in-motion mood, red #E4002B and white light-network the only saturated colour against desaturated daylight, wide-angle perspective, photoreal commercial finish, 16:9. Continuity: the red/white light-network is the anchor, colour and density fixed.

Multiple bundled red fibre-optic light elements stream out from the building into the sky. A red glow blooms on the facade where the first breaks out, then more launch from several points along the facade and roofline through the clip, not all at once. Each has a white-hot bead tip leading, red strands fanning behind as wake; they arc outward and up, easing to a settled glow near the end. Camera cranes slowly and steadily straight up, revealing the building's full height against open sky; the building stays rigid, parallax only. Foreground trees sway; clouds drift slowly.

Locks: facade, sunscreen mesh, roof canopy and columns stay rigid and unwarped, camera parallax only, no bending or leaning. No legible text, logos or signage. Each light element stays red #E4002B with a white bead tip, one bundle.
```

Runs this project's pipeline for one shot, start to finish, instead of the user (or a fresh Claude turn) re-deriving it by hand each time.

## Ground

**PRE:** …lace* — that's `Pan Left` instead.

**Template variant:** default one-line + Positive Locks block — one continuous move, no beat that must land on a specific second, no internal cuts.

## Kling — positive (1184) + negative (306) = 1490/1500

```
One continuous, smooth camera truck to the left along a low row of black liquid-cooling cabinets, straight horizontal path at constant slow speed, low angle at the cabinet-top plane, no rotation or rise. The whole frame is a thermal-camera image the entire shot: deep indigo cool background, coolant plumbing looping off the cabinet tops glowing hot orange to white down the receding row. As it trucks left, the near hot loops and quick-connect fittings slide past frame-left in strong parallax, the row's diagonal opening while the far loops and overhead cable ladder trail behind slower. Slow heat-bloom breathing on the hottest bends, fine thermal grain, soft vignette. The vertical temperature scale with its "26.9" and "20.1" readouts stays fixed at the frame's right edge as shown. Calm premium cooling plant, infrared false-colour, wide-angle barrel character, photoreal, 16:9. Locks: cabinets, fittings, cable ladder, ceiling and floor rigid, parallax only, no warping; loops never snake, sway or writhe, only their heat-bloom changes; the orange-white is thermal heat mapping, not a brand-red glow; scale and numbers frozen, not counting; no legible text or logos; no people.
```

In a bright atrium, an elderly man in a beige shirt and navy vest sits close beside a young woman in an ivory sleeveless dress; he holds a phone, her hand resting over his. The camera dollies slowly forward a short distance, tightening to a shoulders-up two-shot of both faces, then settles and holds; chest level, no crash. Beat 1 (0-4s): both lean toward the phone, faces tense and hopeful; his raised hand lowers onto their joined hands. ## Prompt

```
Photoreal cinematic film still, 16:9. A tender, quiet moment in a bright modern hospital atrium. An elderly man and his granddaughter sit close together on a pale grey mid-century armchair bench with wooden arms, framed left of centre with atrium headroom above. Camera at chest height, slightly below eye level, angled about 45 degrees to their front and favouring the man, normal focal length, minimal distortion, medium shot from the waist up. Shallow depth of field: both faces tack sharp on the same plane, the atrium behind them dissolved into a soft creamy out-of-focus bloom.

The man: late 60s, silver-grey hair swept back, thin round wire-frame glasses, warm beige button-up shirt under a navy knitted sweater-vest. He holds a black smartphone in one hand, screen angled away from camera. The young woman beside him on his left: early 20s, East Asian, shoulder-length tousled dark-brown hair with a side part, warm skin, light natural makeup, ivory sleeveless boat-neck shift dress, a small beaded charm bracelet on one wrist. She leans in against his shoulder, turned toward him and the phone, one hand coming to rest over his hand on the phone. Both look down at the phone, braced and hopeful, breath held, eyes just beginning to shine, the first faint hint of a smile — emotional but pre-peak.

Background, all thrown well out of focus: floor-to-ceiling glass curtain wall with slim mullions, white columns, an upper mezzanine with a glass balustrade, polished pale stone floor, large potted green plants, a wood-panelled reception desk to one side; a few blurred people deep in the background, one crossing with faint motion blur. Warm daylight floods through the glass against cool architectural whites. Naturalistic muted palette, no saturated colour. Shot-on-camera look, natural skin texture and fine detail, gentle film grain, soft contrast. No text, no UI, no numbers, no logos, no signage anywhere; the phone screen is a soft indistinct glow with no readable content and no discernible face.
```

## Stage 2.5 — Director's Brief (reused from H1; deltas: 8s, tears-of-joy register)

```
Director's Brief — H1_2 · Slow Dolly In: Grandfather & Granddaughter Hear the Good News (8s)
Dramatic intent: The moment a long-held fear breaks - an elderly patient and the young woman with
  him take in good news and let themselves feel it, tears of joy coming as she folds into him; the
  viewer should feel relief and tenderness, not connectivity. Brand red #E4002B deliberately absent.
Lead motion: Both faces breaking together on the same beat into teary, relieved smiles - his brows
  lifting, eyes welling and spilling over, a breath, a small trembling smile, a slow blink; her
  face turning toward him, eyes brimming over, her smile breaking through. One lead, not two - both
  react to the same trigger on a single synchronised turn.
Supporting motion: She leans in and folds into a contained hug - upper body turning toward him,
  shoulder-hand sliding around his back, head lowering to his shoulder/temple; he tilts his head
  toward her, clasped hands unclasping, one hand rising to wipe his eye / resting on her forearm.
  A settle, not a lunge - he does not rise. The doctor's news-delivery gesture stays a subordinate
  cue - a hand into the soft foreground, palm opening, a nod - out of focus, no turn.
Ambient motion: Soft daylight from frame right shifting minutely, faint dust, subtle cloth
  movement, the paper roll barely settling; parallax between the pair and the soft background as
  the dolly closes, the doctor's out-of-focus shoulder easing out of frame left.
Camera does: Closes the last bit of distance so the viewer leans out of the exam-room observation
  and into the pair's private relief; as the push develops the doctor's shoulder - the clinical
  framing device - eases out of frame left, leaving us alone with the two of them and the hug. The
  move dignifies and privatises the beat; the deepening background blur keeps the locked brain-scan
  monitor from ever resolving.
Pace: Decelerating - a short, slow, minimal-distance push that eases to a stop and holds as she
  folds into him; no crash, no overshoot. Same register as F28 / F30C / H1 / H3.
```

Runs this project's pipeline for one shot, start to finish, instead of the user (or a fresh Claude turn) re-deriving it by hand each time.

## Stage 2.5 — Director's Brief (reused from H1; deltas: 8s, tears-of-joy, very-slow push)

```
Director's Brief — H1_2 · Very Slow Dolly In: Grandfather & Granddaughter Hear the Good News (8s)
Dramatic intent: The moment a long-held fear breaks - an elderly patient and the young woman with
  him take in good news and let themselves feel it, tears of joy coming as she folds into him; the
  viewer should feel relief and tenderness, not connectivity. Brand red #E4002B deliberately absent.
Lead motion: Both faces breaking together on the same beat into teary, relieved smiles - his brows
  lifting, eyes welling and spilling over, a breath, a small trembling smile, a slow blink; her
  face turning toward him, eyes brimming over, her smile breaking through. One lead, not two - both
  react to the same trigger on a single synchronised turn.
Supporting motion: She leans in and folds into a contained hug - upper body turning toward him,
  shoulder-hand sliding around his back, head lowering to his shoulder/temple; he tilts his head
  toward her, clasped hands unclasping, one hand rising to wipe his eye / resting on her forearm.
  A settle, not a lunge - he does not rise. The doctor's news-delivery gesture stays a subordinate
  cue - a hand into the soft foreground, palm opening, a nod - out of focus, no turn.
Ambient motion: Soft daylight from frame right shifting minutely, faint dust, subtle cloth
  movement, the paper roll barely settling; parallax between the pair and the soft background as
  the dolly creeps in, the doctor's out-of-focus shoulder easing out of frame left.
Camera does: Closes only the last sliver of distance - so slight the subjects barely change size -
  so the viewer leans out of the exam-room observation and into the pair's private relief; the one
  visible travel is the doctor's shoulder drifting off the left edge on parallax, retiring the
  clinical framing device. The deepening background blur keeps the locked brain-scan monitor from
  ever resolving.
Pace: Decelerating from an already very slow start - an almost-imperceptible push that eases to a
  stop and holds as she folds into him; no crash, no overshoot. Same register as F28 / F30C / H1 / H3.
```

Beat 3 (6-8s): standing tall, he looks up as a single glowing red fiber-optic light element flies across the sky above him, white-hot beads travelling along it; a warm, genuine smile forms and holds.
Locks: hat weave, chambray shirt, treeline, horizon and rice rows stay rigid, parallax only; a real crouch-to-standing rise, identity holding, never floating or scaling in place; one red strand only, never lighting the whole sky; the smile warm and natural, never open-mouthed awe or a broad grin; no text or logos.
Avoid: camera drifting off him into bare sky, gaze leaving the sky, hat or shirt morphing, treeline or horizon bending, light element duplicating, strobing or becoming lightning, whole-frame red cast, raindrops, sun breaking through, dolly or crane push, tilted horizon, CGI or 3D-render look.
```

Photoreal architectural photograph of the NeutraDC Cikarang hyperscale data centre:
a long multi-storey block with a charcoal-grey facade of close-spaced vertical
aluminium louvre fins, horizontal banded massing stepping across the elevation,
planted living-wall terraces threaded between the fins, a sculptural red helical
form recessed in the central atrium bay, and a white tensile-steel entrance canopy
at right. Landscaped campus with young trees, clipped hedgerows and a kerbed
access road.

Camera low near the access road, lens pointed upward toward the facade. Wide
establishing shot, the full block spanning the frame. Wide-angle lens perspective:
foreground road and kerb slightly exaggerated, the louvre fins rising tall and
dominant.

A flowing ribbon of red fibre-optic strands — dozens of fine glowing filaments
with bright sparkling nodes along their length — enters from the lower-left
foreground, sweeps in a broad S-curve along the base of the building, then lifts
and funnels into the recessed central atrium, wrapping the red helical sculpture.
The ribbon reads as living data infrastructure; its red exactly matches the
sculpture. Soft red bounce light on the nearest fins and the pavement.

Mid-morning tropical haze, high blue sky with soft clouds, warm neutral daylight.
The red light ribbon is the only saturated colour, glowing against the grey
facade. Sharp detail, natural contrast, 16:9.

Avoid: text, logos, crowds, night scene, extra coloured lighting, distorted
building proportions.

Varied-size soft bokeh circles in dark red (#A5201A) and neutral grey (#C9C9C9) float across a pure flat white field. The camera very slowly dollies straight backward for the entire shot, barely perceptible, with no zoom and no roll, gradually opening more white space around the circles.
Phase 1, free float (0-5s): about 18 circles drift slowly and independently, floating gently as if suspended in still air, gentle parallax, a few easing softly in and out of focus, a faint warm bloom around the red ones. No pattern yet.
Phase 2, formation (5-8.5s): the circles begin to migrate, each gliding smoothly toward a fixed target position, decelerating as a shape emerges - the orbs arranging into the logo icon, a compact angular cluster of dots in staggered columns, red orbs forming the mark and a few grey orbs settling around it.
Phase 3, lock (8.5-10s): the dots hold that arrangement, centered in white space, almost completely still.
Calm, minimal, premium brand-reveal mood, clean motion-graphic finish, flat straight-on frontal view, even soft ambient light, no shadows.
Keep the white field pure flat #FFFFFF with no gradient, vignette or texture; keep every circle a clean soft disc with no warping, morphing, squash or colour shift; the final arrangement is dots only, with no connector lines, no letterforms, no text and no numbers. Avoid flicker, strobing, jitter, snapping, fast motion, lens flare, chromatic aberration, noise and grain. Same circle set throughout.
```

Runs this project's pipeline for one shot, start to finish, instead of the user (or a fresh Claude turn) re-deriving it by hand each time.

## Ground

**PRE:** …nged from the brief.

**Template variant:** default one-line + Positive Locks block, **5s** — same call as the doc; one continuous float, one contained lead beat, nothing timed to a second.

---

## Paste-ready — Kling

**Positive (1195):**

```
A boy in a pale short-sleeve uniform shirt stands by his desk, reciting happily from the open notebook he holds — mouth moving, a bright open smile, eyes up, small chin lifts, a light bounce, tiny grip shifts on the pages. Handheld at human height, medium close-up: a soft low-amplitude sway with tiny organic corrections, holding his face on the upper-left third, quieting toward near-stillness as the moment settles, never fully locking, never a push-in — he stays the same size in frame. At frame right, cropped at the shoulder, the teacher keeps her arms crossed with only the faintest weight shift. Behind him the blurred shapes of classmates stir slightly, none turning to camera. Warm hazy backlight; shallow depth of field — the boy sharp, the room dissolving to soft bokeh, the bright windows blown to circles, no focus pull. Tender, observational, natural classroom daylight, neutral tones, no saturated red, normal focal length, photoreal, 16:9. Locks: the soft background stays soft, nothing resolving into focus or legible text; the notebook's handwriting stays non-legible, no page turn; child count and seating unchanged; the teacher's crossed arms unchanged; no logos or signage.
```

# Shot Prompt — Image-to-Video Pipeline Runner

Runs this project's pipeline for one shot, start to finish, instead of the user (or a fresh Claude turn) re-deriving it by hand each time.

## Ground

**PRE:** …binet row, near corner closest to camera.

Written as an edit/re-render prompt (keep composition, drop the overlay, swap to ultra-wide). Model-agnostic natural language; positive + negative combined ≈1.3k characters, within the 1500 budget.

```
Thermographic false-color view of a data-center hall. Keep the reference's exact camera position and angle: elevated high angle looking down a long black server-cabinet block that runs diagonally from lower-left foreground into the right background, near corner of the cabinets closest to camera, a second cabinet row further back on the right. Glowing incandescent cooling loops arc up from the cabinet tops like heated tubing; overhead cable trays and structural ceiling above; raised-access floor grating lower left. Shot on a 15mm ZEISS Supreme Prime ultra-wide — near-rectilinear, minimal distortion, deep front-to-back focus, everything crisp. Infrared thermal-imaging color mapping: cold indigo-violet environment (#2E1A47 / #3B2063 / #5B2A6E), near-black cool cabinet bodies (#0E0A18), heat as an incandescent ramp on the cabling — deep ember #7A1E00, to orange #D2531A, to amber #F2A83B, to a pale near-white core #FBE7C2. Posed, composed, deliberately arranged industrial frame, cabinet row squared to the lens. No temperature scale bar, no HUD, no on-screen numbers anywhere in frame. 16:9.
```

---

## Per-shot prompt (paste-ready) — Seedance 2.0, ~5s · 1484 chars, negatives folded in

```
Cinematic handheld close-up, warm late-golden-hour light, plaid dress the only warm accent, telephoto compression, shallow depth of field, photoreal finish, 16:9. Continuity: same child, same dress, same hazy Marina Bay skyline.

Slow handheld push-in at operator height, subtle sway, easing toward her face and decelerating to a settle on a tight close-up.
She keeps looking up and off to the left toward the light: one or two slow blinks, her closed-lip smile deepening slightly, a small settle of the head as if on a breath. She does not turn to the camera or look around.
Fine flyaway strands and the longer swept lock of her bob drift in a light breeze, rim-lit hairs catching the backlight.
Behind her the defocused hedge sways, faint haze drifts, warm bokeh shimmer across the soft skyline.
Warm backlight from screen-left, rosy cheeks, natural skin tones.

Locks: Marina Bay Sands, Singapore Flyer, the CBD skyline, horizon and waterline stay soft and rigid, no warping or pulsing. Dress plaid stays stable. No legible text or logos anywhere. Smile stays subtle and closed-lipped, never a broad grin.

Avoid: handheld jitter, lurching push-in, head turning to camera, looking around, broad grin, open-mouthed smile, teeth, exaggerated expression, face distortion, identity drift, warping or bending skyline, background pulsing, flicker, plaid pattern crawling, floating hair, extra limbs, rack-focus hunting, tilted horizon, CGI or 3D-render look, over-sharpened digital look.
```
